I.   INTRODUCTION
     1.   Top Message
          Dear colleagues, business partners, and friends from all sectors of society:

          Over the past year, the global automotive industry has continued to undergo profound transformation. The
          surging waves of electrification, intelligentization, and greening are accelerating the reshaping of market
          dynamics, while consumer demands grow increasingly diverse. At the same time, topics such as climate change,
          resource conservation, social responsibility, and governance have evolved beyond mere societal consensus to
          become critical benchmarks for assessing whether a company possesses long-term vitality and value creation
          capabilities. In this context, I am honored to present our latest Environmental, Social, and Governance (ESG)
          Report on behalf of the Autostreets’ Board of Directors, demonstrating our insights, practices, and commitments
          on the path to sustainable development.

          From the first hammer strike at the 2014 Shanghai auction to our current nationwide service network
          facilitating hundreds of thousands of efficient and transparent transactions, we have unwaveringly pursued
          our founding mission to reshape China’s used vehicle transaction process. Every step of our growth is rooted
          in a deep understanding of and unwavering commitment to China’s automotive distribution industry. We have
          always believed that true industry leaders must shoulder broad social and environmental responsibilities while
          creating commercial value. This report not only provides a systematic overview of our environmental, social,
          and governance (ESG) practices over the past year, but also represents our solemn commitment to our vision of
          becoming Asia’s most sustainable used vehicle trading platform for the future.

          We fully recognize that the platform nature of Autostreets endows us with a unique and pivotal role
          in driving the industry’s green transformation. We are not direct emission reducers in manufacturing,
          but through efficient connectivity, we have become key enablers of the circular economy. We take
          environmental impacts into account before conducting our business. As a used vehicle transaction service
          provider, we do not engage in any physical manufacturing activities, and therefore, we do not emit a significant
          amount of greenhouse gases, wastes, or pollutants from our business operations. In 2025, our platform
          facilitated the efficient circulation of about 141,000 used vehicles. Every successful transaction represents an
          instance of efficient resource reuse and a potential substitution for carbon emissions generated during new
          vehicle production. Through our integrated online-offline auction model, we have significantly shortened vehicle
          turnover cycles and reduced intermediary steps, thereby lowering the industry’s overall energy consumption and
          emissions from the distribution side. We have also internalized green philosophies into our own operations. In
          2025, we achieved significant results with electricity consumption decreasing by 13.70% year-on-year and water
          consumption decreasing by 11.62% year-on-year. Behind this lies our vigorous promotion of digital and paperless
          office practices, widespread adoption of energy-efficient equipment, and enhanced awareness of water and
          electricity conservation among all employees.

          We believe that the sustainable development of the industry depends on individual growth. In 2025,
          we provided employees with a total of 3,344.5 hours of training, covering topics ranging from new employee
          orientation and assessor certification to cutting-edge AI technology applications. We collaborate closely with
          industry associations, participated in the release of the Electric Vehicle Auction Inspection and Appraisal
          Specifications, and continue to cultivate standardized professionals for the industry. Our service network now
          spans over 8,600 partner automobile sales service shops and 60,000 downstream customers nationwide. By
          providing complementary services such as financing and logistics, we promote balanced market development.




                                                                    Autostreets Development Limited / Annual Report 2025   51
ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND GOVERNANCE REPORT

         Being a listed company entails stricter requirements and broader responsibilities. We regard excellent
         corporate governance as the cornerstone for steady and sustainable progress. The Board of Directors
         effectively fulfills its oversight responsibilities for ESG initiatives. Among the nine directors, 33.33% are female,
         representing diverse professional backgrounds. We strictly comply with listing rules, convening nine shareholder
         meetings and various board meetings in 2025 to ensure scientific and efficient decision-making. We have
         embedded risk management and compliance awareness into our very DNA. Through a three-line defense system,
         we strengthen internal controls and audit oversight; rigorously manage 8,692 suppliers by incorporating anti-
         corruption, environmental protection, and social responsibility requirements into cooperation agreements; we
         prioritize data security and privacy protection, aiming for zero data breaches by 2025 while continuously refining
         emergency response plans.

         We are also honored to see that these efforts have garnered both internal and external recognition:
         Wind ESG rating has risen to Grade A, and we have been awarded the highest AAA rating for auction
         enterprises, the “Shanghai Auction Industry Innovation Award”, and other honors. This is not only an
         affirmation of our past achievements but also a driving force for our future endeavors.

         The road is long and challenging, but with each step forward, we draw nearer to our destination. ESG practices
         are a never-ending climb. Looking ahead, we will continue to deepen the application of digital and intelligent
         technologies to enhance efficiency across the entire value chain. We will persistently expand our capabilities
         in new energy used vehicle services to lead the way in green circulation. We will continuously refine our ESG
         governance framework, embedding sustainability more deeply into our corporate strategy and daily operations.

  2.   Basics of the Report
       This report is the 2025 ESG report released by Autostreets Development Limited (stock code: 2443.HK), aiming
       to respond to stakeholder expectations and comprehensively demonstrate the Company’s philosophy, actions,
       and achievements in environmental, social, and governance. This report has been reviewed and approved by the
       Board of Directors to ensure that there are no untrue records, misleading statements, or material omissions in its
       contents.

       (1)   Reporting Principles
             This report is prepared in accordance with the principles of “Materiality,” “Quantification,” and
             “Consistency” as set out in the Appendix C2 of the Main Board Listing Rules, Environmental, Social and
             Governance Reporting Guide (“ESG Guide of HKEX”), and the Guidelines issued by the Hong Kong
             Exchanges and Clearing Limited (hereinafter referred to as “HKEX”). It is primarily determined based on the
             assessment of material topics and stakeholder feedback.

             “Materiality” principle: The Company comprehensively identifies key stakeholder groups, conducts an in-
             depth analysis of substantive ESG topics that are material or relevant to the Company and stakeholders, and
             makes full disclosure in this report accordingly.

             “Quantification” principle: Key performance indicators disclosed in this report are scientifically measured to
             ensure data accuracy and verifiability, enabling readers to clearly understand the Company’s environmental,
             social, and governance performance.

             “Consistency” Principle: Unless otherwise specified, the statistical methods used for information disclosure
             in this report are consistent with those employed in previous years.

       (2)   Period Covered
             This report covers the period from January 1st, 2025, to December 31st, 2025. Some of the statements and
             data are not within the above period and will be explained when involved.

       (3)   Organizations Covered
             The reporting entity is Autostreets Development Limited and its subsidiaries. The financial data used is
             from the Company’s annual report, and other data is from official documents and statistical reports of the
             Company (previous years’ data should be based on the disclosure in this report).

       (4)   Definitions
             For the convenience of presentation and readability, “Autostreets Development Limited” is also referred to
             in the report as “Autostreets”, “the Company”, or “we”.

     3.   Corporate Profile
          (1) Company Overview
              Autostreets Development Limited, listed on the Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited in
              May 2024 (stock code: 2443.HK), is a leading automotive transaction service platform in China established
              in June 2014 with headquarters in Shanghai. The Company was jointly established and funded by
              Manheim and SINO RICH INVESTMENT under Cox Automotive of the United States, and the core top 100
              dealership groups led by the China Automobile Dealers Association. Since its establishment, Autostreets
              has shouldered the responsibility of promoting the development of the used vehicle industry. As a
              transaction service platform focused on enhancing used vehicle business value, Autostreets is committed
              to transforming China’s used vehicle transaction processes. Through its online-offline integrated auction
              model and comprehensive value-added services, the Company has developed an end-to-end standardized
              used vehicle transaction solution for buyers and sellers, leading China’s used vehicle industry toward
              standardization, efficiency, and transparency. Autostreets’ services cover used vehicle auctions, used vehicle
              value-added services, used vehicle acquisition and sales arrangements, exhibition business, and other
              automotive-related services.




54    Autostreets Development Limited / Annual Report 2025

          To date, Autostreets’ service network spans across all provinces in mainland China except Tibet, establishing
          a multi-level, cross-regional comprehensive service network system for national used vehicle auctions. This
          network not only has extensive reach but, through its robust service capabilities and professional expertise,
          has successfully established deep partnerships with many dealership groups. It continuously injects strong
          momentum into the value enhancement of Autostreets’ used vehicle businesses and makes Autostreets an
          industry benchmark.

     4.   ESG Key Performance Data
          In 2025, the Company made significant achievements in a number of areas, including economic, environmental,
          social, and governance, demonstrating its excellence and strong commitment to sustainable development.

          Economic performance: In 2025, the Company achieved operating revenue of RMB271 million, with about
          141,000 used vehicles transacted and about 310,000 vehicles listed for auction, representing a year-on-year
          reduction rate of 21.4% and 22.0%, respectively. The transaction success rate (the percentage of used vehicle
          transactions to the number of used vehicles listed for auction) was approximately 45.4%. Over 9,000 new energy
          vehicles were sold, with their transaction volume accounting for 12.5% of the transaction volume from traditional
          business.

          Environmental performance: In 2025, the Company’s total water consumption was about 8,000 tons,
          representing a year-on-year decrease of 11.62%, equivalent to planting 574 trees; total electricity consumption
          was about 890,000 kilowatt-hours, representing a year-on-year reduction of 13.70%; the Company invested
          about RMB60,000 in environmental R&D.

          Social Performance: The Company actively fulfills its responsibilities to employees, customers, and communities,
          committed to building trusted partnerships and driving inclusive growth. As of the end of 2025, its network
          covers 331 cities, partners with over 8,600 4S stores, and serves more than 60,000 downstream customers.
          Female employees account for 26.00% of the Company’s workforce, and the total employee training duration
          reached 3,344.50 hours. The Company places great importance on consumer rights protection, customer
          relationships, and product/service quality. In 2025, the customer complaint resolution rate was 100.00%, and
          customer satisfaction was 99.90%.

          Governance performance: Female employees account for 26.00% of Autostreets’ workforce, and there are 3
          female directors, accounting for 33.33% of the Board of Directors, demonstrating the Company’s efforts in
          gender diversity.

II.   PACESETTER IN GREEN TRAVEL AND LOW-CARBON DEVELOPMENT DRIVEN BY
      THE CIRCULAR ECONOMY
      1.   Ecological Protection for “Dual Carbon” Goal Accomplishment
           (1) GHG Management Initiatives and Results
                1)    GHG Emission Reduction Targets
                      To continuously improve atmospheric environmental quality, the government departments have
                      increasingly strict regulations on the environmental compliance of used vehicles. The Company
                      actively responds to national policies, strictly implements emission testing standards for used vehicles,
                      and establishes a complete environmental testing information file for each traded vehicle, ensuring
                      their environmental compliance and avoiding pollution transfer risks from the circulation of vehicles
                      with excessive emissions at the source. Meanwhile, it comprehensively integrates the concept of
                      sustainable development into its entire operational process and effectively improves the overall
                      environmental governance of the industry through standard and systematic green services. In 2025,
                      the Company invested RMB63,392.62 in environmental protection.

                       The Company is committed to achieving effective control over total greenhouse gas emissions and
                       ensuring that the total emissions, including Scope 1, Scope 2, and Scope 3 emissions, do not exceed
                       the 2025 baseline levels. At the same time, we will continue to strengthen systematic management of
                       key pollutants such as volatile organic compounds (VOCs), nitrogen oxides (NOx), and fine particulate
                       matter (PM2.5), ensuring their emission intensities consistently meet and go beyond the latest
                       national standards. Through a range of initiatives, such as advancing the improvement of energy
                       efficiency and optimizing end-of-pipe treatment systems, we will achieve coordinated control and
                       dynamic equilibrium between total emissions and emission intensity in the course of the sustained
                       development of our business.

                       In terms of energy conservation and carbon reduction, the Company is systematically promoting
                       refined energy management, strictly enforcing environmental standards in core operations like
                       vehicle testing, and continuously exploring the application of innovative technologies to improve
                       resource utilization efficiency. We are committed to implementing the above initiatives to reduce total
                       greenhouse gas emissions, including Scope 1, Scope 2, and Scope 3 emissions, by a large margin
                       during our operations, and minimize our impact on the climate and environment, thereby leading the
                       green and low-carbon transformation of the testing industry and promoting deep integration and
                       collaborative development of corporate sustainable development and ecological and environmental
                       protection.

                  3)    GHG Management Systems
                        In order to effectively deepen greenhouse gas reduction initiatives, strengthen the whole-process
                        carbon emission management, and push forward corporate transformation toward green and low-
                        carbon model against the macro backdrop of active global efforts to advance climate governance and
                        green transformation, Autostreets strictly abides by the Environmental Protection Law of the People’s
                        Republic of China, Air Pollution Prevention and Control Law of the People’s Republic of China,
                        Administrative Measures for Carbon Emission Trading (Trial) and other national laws and regulations,
                        and proactively integrates carbon management into corporate strategy and daily operations to
                        systematically promote emission reduction practices.

                  4)    GHG Emission Reduction Measures
                        In advancing low-carbon office practices, we actively guide all employees to embrace the concept of
                        a green office. On one hand, we take initiatives such as double-sided printing and reducing paper
                        usage to minimize resource consumption for office purposes at the source. On the other hand, we
                        comprehensively promote energy-efficient office equipment and electrical appliances, such as energy-
                        saving air conditioners and computers, to continuously reduce energy consumption in office areas
                        and thereby decrease corresponding greenhouse gas emissions. Additionally, we vigorously promote
                        green commuting practices, encourage employees to prioritize low-carbon transportation options for
                        business travel, and implement low-carbon emission requirements across the board, including office
                        trivialities, in a bid to realize deep integration of green and low-carbon principles into all aspects of
                        daily work.

           (2)    Water Resources Management
                  1)  Water Resources Management Objectives
                      Autostreets attaches great importance to water resources protection and continues to improve water
                      utilization efficiency and effectively reduce water consumption in production and operation through
                      multiple measures such as optimizing water-using facilities, strengthening whole-process monitoring
                      of water use, and conducting specialized training on water conservation for employees. The Company
                      uses municipal water supply for production in a unified manner, which is stable and reliable, and
                      the water quality is up to the relevant standard. There are no water safety risks at present or in the
                      foreseeable future. It also steadfastly adheres to its primary responsibility for water conservation, and
                      continuously advances the implementation of water-saving measures to make its due contribution in
                      earnest to water protection and sustainable utilization.

                        water-saving toilets, and other devices. All this effectively reduces daily water consumption intensity,
                        integrates water conservation practices into every aspect of daily operations, and achieves intensive,
                        efficient utilization of water resources.

     2.   Goal-Driven Climate Action
          (1) “Carbon Peaking and Carbon Neutrality” Goals
               In active response to the strategic deployment in China’s Fourteenth Five-Year Plan for the Development
               of the Circular Economy regarding the promotion of the “Internet + Second-hand product” model and
               the efficient recycling of resources, the Company embraces the important opportunity for the used vehicle
               market to realize standardized development and grow in scale. It proactively grasps the direction of this
               policy, takes efforts to ensure environmental compliance of used vehicles with the focus on enhancing
               the standardization and circulation efficiency of used vehicle transactions and effectively extending vehicle
               service life, and also promotes the recycling of resources such as steel and non-ferrous metals, thereby
               making an important contribution to the circular economy in the transportation sector.

    (2)   Climate Risk Management and TCFD Framework Response
          The Task Force on Climate-Related Financial Disclosure (TCFD), established by the Financial Stability Board
          (FSB), issued a globally influential framework of information disclosure recommendations in 2017. This
          framework aims to provide a voluntary disclosure standard for businesses, investors, and policymakers to
          systematically identify, assess, and manage financial risks arising from climate change and capture strategic
          opportunities during the transition toward the low-carbon economy. By promoting high-quality, comparable
          climate information transparency, the TCFD framework is becoming a key bridge connecting climate action
          with financial decision-making, which guides capital flows toward more sustainable and resilient economic
          models.

     3.   Circular Vehicle Ecosystem for Green, Low-Carbon, and Circular Development
          The circular economy centers on efficient resource utilization and recycling, transforming the traditional linear
          development model of “resource acquisition-product manufacturing-waste disposal” into a circular production
          and consumption pattern. This transformation not only creates greater economic value and more employment
          opportunities and enhances the overall resilience of social development, but also helps reduce greenhouse gas
          emissions at the source and reduces waste generation and environmental pollution, which effectively addresses
          global ecological challenges such as climate change and biodiversity loss, and ultimately achieves common
          progress in economic development and ecological protection.

          The Company places high importance on biodiversity conservation and strictly adheres to domestic ecological
          protection laws and regulations. In the future, it will take into account the operational characteristics of the
          automotive transaction service industry, and on this basis, systematically assess the potential impacts of its
          business activities on the ecological environment and biodiversity, continuously strengthen the cultivation of
          employees’ awareness of ecological protection, and integrate biodiversity conservation requirements into all
          aspects of daily operations and management, in a bid to minimize the potential impacts of its business activities
          on surrounding ecosystems and biodiversity, steadily advance the common progress in biodiversity conservation
          and corporate development, and fulfill the Company’s social responsibility in ecological protection.

          In the automotive industry, establishing a circular vehicle ecosystem is an important approach to implementing
          the circular economy concept and advancing low-carbon development. Specialized automotive transaction
          platforms serve as a crucial carrier for realizing the circular vehicle ecosystem and empowering low-carbon and
          circular development. Leveraging this transaction platform’s functional layout and resource advantages, the
          Company can actively promote low-carbon and circular development of the automotive industry through multiple
          dimensions, specifically:

          1.    Guide consumers to prioritize existing used vehicles in the market over purchasing new ones, thereby
                reducing resource consumption and carbon emissions associated with new vehicle production. According to
                data from the International Energy Agency (IEA), the transportation sector accounts for approximately 23%
                of global greenhouse gas emissions. The processing and manufacturing of raw materials such as steel and
                aluminum products represent hotspots with significant carbon emissions in the automotive supply chain.
                In 2025, our platform facilitated the successful transactions of about 141,000 used vehicles. Compared
                to consumers’ purchase of new vehicles, these transactions significantly reduced the proportion of global
                greenhouse gas emissions attributable to the transportation sector.




70    Autostreets Development Limited / Annual Report 2025
ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND GOVERNANCE REPORT

        2.    Based on specialized operations and digital tools of the platform, optimize the used vehicle transaction
              service system, enhance overall transaction efficiency of used vehicles, and thereby accelerate the circulation
              of automotive resources. Through continuous upgrades of technology systems of our auction platform,
              we have strengthened risk control capabilities across critical stages from entrusted auction, transaction
              matchmaking, online payment, to logistics order placement, and have improved our service quality
              while optimizing cost structures. We have pioneered building an integrated online-offline auction model
              supporting synchronous auction and online auction, thereby increasing vehicle exposure and improving our
              transaction success rate. This integrated transaction platform enables us to efficiently connect upstream
              suppliers with downstream buyers in the used vehicle market, facilitating numerous transparent and prompt
              auctions. Furthermore, through strict control over vehicle testing standards and the implementation of
              efficient transaction models, we have enhanced the circulation safety and recyclability of vehicles on our
              platform, which strongly promotes the sustainable development of the circular economy specific to used
              vehicles.

        3.    Through standardized vehicle testing, appraisal, and circulation services, we effectively extend the actual
              service life of vehicles and directly reduce the quantity of end-of-life vehicles. When end-of-life vehicles are
              disposed of, improper management may cause environmental pollution from leakage of refrigerant and
              disposal of hazardous waste such as discarded storage batteries and electronic components. Therefore, it
              is of significant environmental significance to promote the recycling of used vehicles compared to direct
              disposal.

        The government has enacted and implemented relevant laws and regulations, such as the Circular Economy
        Promotion Law of the People’s Republic of China and the Law of the People’s Republic of China on the Prevention
        and Control of Solid Waste Pollution, which guide and regulate the processes of end-of-life vehicle disposal
        so that the vehicle scrapping process is more efficient and environmentally friendly. With this policy, if used
        vehicles achieve recycling and extended service life through this platform, they will receive support of a more
        comprehensive regulatory framework and mature technology conditions when entering into the final scrapping
        stage in the future, which realizes vehicle disposal in a more environmentally friendly, standardized, and resource-
        efficient manner and thereby truly advance efficient resource utilization and ecological circular development
        throughout the entire automotive lifecycle.

III. ADHERENCE TO PEOPLE-CENTERED MANAGEMENT AND RESPONSIBLE
     OPERATION FOR SUSTAINABLE GROWTH
   1.   Employment
        Autostreets strictly complies with relevant national laws and regulations in employment and
        management, consistently upholds the core principles of lawfulness, fairness, and equity, and is
        committed to creating a high-quality working environment and a broad career development platform
        for all employees. In strict compliance with relevant laws and regulations, such as the Labor Law of the People’s
        Republic of China, the Labor Contract Law of the People’s Republic of China, and the Employment Promotion
        Law of the People’s Republic of China, Autostreets protects the legitimate rights and interests of employees in
        all aspects and from multiple dimensions. In addition, Autostreets has established a comprehensive and sound
        remuneration structure with diverse benefits to fully stimulate employees’ potential and empower them to realize
        personal value in tandem with the achievement of corporate development.




                                                                  Autostreets Development Limited / Annual Report 2025    71
ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND GOVERNANCE REPORT

         (1)   Employee Recruitment
               Autostreets always puts human resources management at the strategic core of corporate
               development and takes it as a vital foundation for stable development and long-term growth.
               The Company has established a scientific and comprehensive institutional framework and
               implemented a standardized and efficient recruitment process to strengthen talent support
               and build a solid talent foundation for its sustainable development. Pursuant to multi-dimensional
               standard systems for quality management and occupational health, Autostreets has established a
               comprehensive human resources management framework. The core systems include key regulations such
               as the Employee Management System, Remuneration Management System, and Performance Management
               System, and on this basis, the Recruitment Management System and Employee Promotion and Transfer
               System are kept refining, which clarify the recruitment process, job requirements, evaluation criteria for
               hiring, onboarding procedures, and other key aspects to ensure the standardization, rigor, and transparency
               of the recruitment work. During the recruitment implementation phase, the Company widely publishes
               recruitment information through multiple channels and in various forms, encompassing recruitment
               websites, social media platforms, university-industry collaborations, and employee referrals, to attract high-
               quality talents and gather industry elites and thereby inject innovative vitality and talent momentum into the
               Company’s sustainable development.

         (2)   Employee Diversity
               Autostreets consistently adheres to the core principles of inclusion and diversity, actively building
               an energetic talent team with diversity. At present, the Company has a total of 600 employees, and
               the composition is multidimensionally diversified. From the perspective of gender distribution, there are 444
               male employees (74.00%) and 156 female employees (26.00%). Employees of different genders perform
               their own duties and achieve complementarity for common development, thereby jointly advancing
               corporate development. With regard to employment form, there are 593 full-time employees (98.83%),
               serving as the core pillar of corporate development, and their stable services ensure the continuous
               advancement of our business. Part-time employees totaled 7 (1.16%), and their flexible collaboration
               model provides effective support for the Company’s response to business fluctuations. In terms of age
               structure, 69 employees are aged under 30 (11.50%). This dynamic and innovative young workforce injects
               continuous creative energy into the Company. 500 employees are aged between 30 and 50 (83.33%),
               forming the backbone of corporate development. Their solid professional expertise and extensive practical
               experience solidify the foundation for stable business operations. 31 employees are aged over 50 (5.00%),
               providing crucial guidance for corporate development through their profound industry accumulation
               and invaluable practical wisdom. In terms of educational background, there are 384 employees with
               an education below the bachelor’s degree (64.00%), 197 with the bachelor’s degree (32.83%), 19
               with the master’s degree, doctoral degree or above (3.17%). This diverse educational structure meets
               the specialized demands of various positions, forming a well-structured talent pipeline. Geographically,
               the talent network of Autostreets spans all provinces in mainland China except Tibet, with employees
               coming from diverse regions: 290 from East China (48.33%), 80 from Central China (13.33%), 82 from
               Southwest China (13.67%), 70 from North China (11.67%), 25 from Northeast China (4.17%), 35 from
               Northwest China (5.83%), 17 from South China (2.83%), and 1 from the U.S. (0.17%). Employees from
               different regions bring unique cultural backgrounds and ways of thinking, which blend together to form an
               inclusive corporate culture and lay a solid foundation for the Company’s country-wide business layout and
               expansion.

         (3)   Employee Promotion
               Autostreets always prioritizes employee career development and is committed to establishing
               clear, fair, and transparent promotion channels that provide equal opportunities for continuous
               growth and advancement of all employees. In particular, the Company has put in place the Autostreets
               Employee Promotion and Transfer System and other normative documents, which clarify the operating
               procedures and implementation rules for employee promotion and transfer to establish a clear career
               development path for employees and help them realize their personal values and career goals through the
               corporate platform. To meet the role adaptation and competency enhancement needs of newly promoted
               managers, Autostreets tailors exclusive training programs for them at different levels. The training curricula
               cover core dimensions such as management philosophy development, team-building methodologies, and
               effective communication skills. Through systematic training and practical guidance, these programs help the
               new managers rapidly enhance their comprehensive management capabilities, secure a smooth transition
               into their roles, and efficiently fulfill their management responsibilities.

         (4)   Employee Remuneration and Benefits
               Autostreets unswervingly integrates employee well-being into the core of corporate development
               and strives to create a healthy, harmonious, and people-centered working environment. In
               terms of working hour management, the Company strictly operates a standard working hour system and
               implements the workweek that follows 5 days a week and 8 hours a day, to ensure that employees have
               sufficient rest time and help realize a work-life balance. As a safeguard of their right to vacation, employees
               enjoy statutory holidays such as two-day weekend, statutory holidays, marriage leave, bereavement leave,
               maternity leave, paternity leave, etc., and the Company also implements the annual leave system in strict
               compliance with the national Regulations on Paid Annual Leave for Employees and the Measures for the
               Implementation of Paid Annual Leave for Enterprise Employees, to ensure the satisfaction of employees’
               needs for rest and recuperation, alleviate work-related stress, and continuously enhance their quality of life.

  2.   Employee Health and Safety
       Autostreets has always placed employee health and safety at the core of its operations, regarding it
       as the cornerstone of the Company’s sustainable development. We strictly comply with the Labour
       Law of the People’s Republic of China and domestic and international occupational health and
       safety management standards, including GB/T45001-2020 and ISO45001, and have established and
       continuously improved a robust and systematic occupational health and safety management system.
       Through concrete measures such as scientifically planning vehicle traffic routes, eliminating mixed pedestrian-
       vehicle zones, and installing weather-protected assessment tents, we have effectively safeguarded on-site
       operational safety. From 2023 to 2025, the Company has achieved three consecutive years with zero work-
       related fatalities and zero lost workdays due to occupational injuries — a testament to its steadfast commitment
       and continuous progress in occupational health and safety. We continuously enhance our management
       mechanisms, regularly conduct safety training, and consistently improve the working environment to foster safer,
       healthier, and more comfortable working conditions for employees. Meanwhile, the Company strictly adheres to
       national policies by fully paying basic medical insurance contributions for all employees, ensuring 100% medical
       insurance coverage, and comprehensively strengthening the health protection safety net for employees.

  3.   Employee Development and Training
       Employee development and training are integral components of Autostreets’ strategic framework.
       The Company regards employees’ professional growth and capability enhancement as a core driver
       for achieving sustainable development. In 2025, the Company invested a total of RMB89,640.46 in training,
       building a high-quality and diverse learning resource system to support all employees in continuously enhancing
       their professional competencies and overall capabilities. At the institutional level, the Company has formulated
       and implemented specialized policies, including the Autostreets Employee Training Management System and
       the Autostreets — Safety Training and Education Strategies, establishing a solid institutional foundation for
       the standardized implementation of training programs. In terms of resource allocation, the Company carefully
       considers the differentiated needs of employees across various roles and categories. Training content is
       systematically categorized into general, professional, management, competency, risk control, and onboarding
       tracks. Tailored training curricula are then designed based on the role-specific characteristics and business
       development needs of employees at all levels, effectively promoting a high degree of alignment between
       employee capabilities and job requirements, and fully unlocking talent potential.

       In 2025, the Company delivered a total of 13 training sessions throughout the year, including thematic programs
       such as onboarding training, appraiser certification training, and AI-focused training. The total length of training
       was 3,344.5 hours, and the total number of trainees was 1,527, with a per capita length of 2.19 hours; 1,279
       men were trained, with a training participation rate of 83.76%, and a per capita length of 2.08 hours; and 248
       women were trained, with a training participation rate of 16.24%, and a per capita length of 2.78 hours. According
       to employee classes, 8 senior management trainees accounted for a training participation rate of 0.52% and a
       per capita length of 4.13 hours, mainly focusing on strategic leadership and front-line trends in the industry; 266
       middle management trainees accounted for a training participation rate of 17.42% and a per capita length of
       3.55 hours, further strengthening management skills and teamwork; 1,253 grass-roots employees accounted for
       a training participation rate of 82.06% and a per capita length of 1.89 hours, focusing on enhancing professional
       skills and job competency. The Company has established a comprehensive training system that spans all
       organizational levels and covers every position, ensuring that all employees have access to learning resources and
       development support tailored to their job grades and responsibilities.

  4.   Labor Standards
       Autostreets has always placed the fulfillment of corporate social responsibility and adherence to
       compliant operations at the core of its development, consistently guiding all stages of human resources
       management with the principles of fair and lawful employment practices. The Company upholds an open
       and inclusive talent philosophy and explicitly prohibits any form of discrimination — including, but not limited
       to, nationality, race, gender, and religious belief — in its Personnel Recruitment Management Rules, thereby
       fostering a fair, diverse, and inclusive workplace environment. The Company maintains a zero-tolerance policy
       toward serious violations such as forced labor and child labor. In practice, the Human Resources department
       verifies the employee’s identity documents prior to onboarding to confirm their actual age and proactively
       communicates with them to ensure that their decision to join the Company is voluntary, informed, and made of
       their own free will. Meanwhile, the Company guides employees in signing labor contracts and other onboarding
       documents to ensure that all employment practices strictly comply with national laws and regulations. During the
       reporting period, the Company did not engage in any incidents of child labor or forced labor. This underscores
       Autostreets’ firm commitment to lawful employment practices and corporate social responsibility, demonstrating
       both a principled stance and concrete actions that effectively safeguard employee rights and ensure compliance
       in business operations.

       Autostreets has established a comprehensive human rights risk prevention and response mechanism,
       and will implement a systematic response protocol whenever incidents of illegal employment practices
       — such as child labor or forced labor — are identified. First, the Company will immediately halt the relevant
       activity, remove the individuals involved from their original work environment, and provide safe, protective
       isolation to effectively safeguard their personal safety and fundamental rights. Subsequently, the Company will
       proactively report the incident to the local labor inspection or relevant law enforcement authorities as required by
       law, fully submit all pertinent materials — including information on the individuals involved, recruitment records,
       and attendance documentation — and actively cooperate with any subsequent investigation. For any affected
       child laborers or victims of forced labor, the Company will arrange professional health assessments, proactively
       contact their legal guardians and family members, facilitate their safe return home, and — in the case of child
       laborers who have not completed compulsory education — assist them in returning to school or provide support
       for their continued education. Meanwhile, the Company will initiate an internal investigation to thoroughly
       review any gaps in its recruitment and management processes, enhance legal and regulatory training for HR and
       relevant personnel, improve internal whistleblowing channels, and strictly protect the confidentiality and safety of
       whistleblowers. In addition, the Company will duly accept administrative penalties imposed by relevant authorities
       and bear any fines that may arise; should any harm result from the violation, the Company will proactively cover
       medical expenses and fulfill its corresponding compensation obligations. Through these multi-tiered response
       measures, Autostreets is committed to ensuring full compliance across all business operations and safeguarding
       the legitimate rights and interests of every worker.

     5.     Supply Chain Management
            Autostreets places great emphasis on the pivotal role of supply chain management in the used vehicle
            circulation sector. Guided by sustainable development principles and grounded in compliant operations,
            the Company integrates green practices throughout the entire supply chain lifecycle. It is committed to
            building an efficient, transparent, and environmentally responsible supply chain management system,
            actively advancing green procurement, and collaborating with upstream and downstream partners to
            jointly drive the industry toward a sustainable future. To operationalize the above strategy, the Company
            has established internal policies such as the Supplier Admission Measures, creating a comprehensive end-to-end
            management system that covers supplier admission, due diligence, performance evaluation, and exit mechanisms.
            This framework establishes a closed-loop process — from procurement need assessment and supplier selection
            to contract execution monitoring — ensuring both partnership quality and compliance with regulatory and
            operational standards. Leveraging this standardized management system, the Company has built a multi-tiered
            supplier network comprising used vehicle trading companies, 4S dealership groups, and institutional clients,
            totaling over 8,600 suppliers, with regional distribution as follows: East China 39.93%, North China 13.47%,
            Southwest China 13.44%, Central China 11.87%, South China 9.92%, Northwest China 6.25%, and Northeast
            China 5.12%5. Moreover, in contracts with upstream and downstream partners, the Company not only includes
            standard requirements regarding employee safety and property protection, service quality, and data handling,
            but also incorporates dedicated clauses on anti-bribery, anti-corruption, and anti-money laundering, requiring
            suppliers to formally acknowledge and sign off on these provisions. Through rigorous controls at every stage, the
            Company ensures that suppliers comply with applicable laws and regulations across environmental, social, and
            governance (ESG) dimensions, effectively mitigating potential supply chain risks and reinforcing the foundation
            for sustainable and resilient business growth.

            (1)    Collaborative Industry Development
                   Autostreets has always been committed to advancing the collective growth of the industry, actively
                   partnering with stakeholders across the ecosystem and participating in key industry events to energize the
                   used vehicle sector. By moving in step with the industry, the Company strives for mutual reinforcement and
                   shared prosperity — working together toward a broader and more sustainable future.

                   From October 22nd to 23rd, 2025, Autostreets was invited to attend the Third China Motor Vehicle Auction
                   Industry Conference, hosted by the China Association of Auctioneers. Themed “Industry Advancement,
                   Technology Shaping the Future,” the conference brought together nearly 400 industry experts. At the
                   conference, Autostreets’ core team engaged in in-depth exchanges. The Company was honored with
                   multiple awards in recognition of its outstanding performance, including being named one of the “Top 3
                   Companies by Both Auction Transaction Volume and Value in China’s Motor Vehicle Auction Industry for
                   2024” and receiving the title of “2024 Outstanding Enterprise in Motor Vehicle Auctions — Influential
                   Brand”. Several of its regional subsidiaries were also recognized, fully demonstrating Autostreets’ leadership
                   in the industry. Meanwhile, Autostreets also participated in the launch of the industry’s first Technical
                   Specification for Inspection and Valuation of Electric Vehicles in Auctions, and signed investment and
                   cooperation agreements at the conference for the Used Vehicle Export Auction Service Platform, further
                   expanding its footprint in international online trading. Moreover, a representative of the Company delivered
                   a keynote speech titled “Upholding Integrity, Embracing Innovation” at the main forum, presenting
                   the insight that “standardized services should be built within a fragmented market, and certainty of
                   value created amid volatile cycles.” This perspective offered valuable strategic direction for the future
                   development of the industry.

      Autostreets warmly welcomed the association leadership’s visit and expressed sincere gratitude for the

    (2)   Whole Life Cycle Management of Supply Chain
          In today’s challenging business environment, effective supplier management is a critical enabler
          of corporate sustainable development. To this end, we have established a comprehensive institutional
          framework covering the entire supplier lifecycle, including core policies such as the Admission for Licensed
          Suppliers, ensuring that every stage — from supplier onboarding to contract fulfillment — is governed by
          clear rules and supported by comprehensive documentation. At the supplier admission stage, a stringent
          screening process is in place, mandating the proposal of multiple qualified legal-entity suppliers (excluding
          individual businesses), accompanied by comprehensive corporate documentation for each. The application
          process undergoes multi-level reviews, and the evaluation panel strictly enforces entry criteria to ensure
          that shortlisted suppliers possess high-caliber service capabilities. In the contract review stage, a multi-
          layered control mechanism is implemented: in addition to approvals from the relevant business departments
          and management, the Legal and Internal Audit departments are concurrently engaged to provide written
          assessments of potential risks. The Audit Department also implements end-to-end oversight across all key
          stages — including tendering, contract signing, and contract performance — to provide proactive risk
          warnings, exercise real-time control, and conduct post-implementation evaluations. During the cooperation
          period, the rights and responsibilities of both parties are clearly defined through standardized OA workflows
          and the signing of agreements. The agreements impose strict requirements on vehicle inspection standards
          to ensure that only vehicles in excellent condition are listed for auction, thereby indirectly improving the
          environmental performance of used vehicles. Meanwhile, we continuously monitor operational data to
          dynamically oversee supplier performance. With this robust management system in place, the Company
          has not only ensured the stable operation of its vehicle registration and transfer facilitation services
          and strengthened its market competitiveness, but also established a replicable best practice in supplier
          management for the industry to follow.

                a healthy and sustainable supply chain ecosystem. To this end, the Company has established a
                systematic set of management principles for external procurement and partnerships, setting clear standards
                on integrity, social responsibility, and environmental compliance to guide and govern supplier conduct,
                jointly fostering a transparent and well-regulated business environment. At the same time, we have actively

                and specific requirements for reporting anti-corruption concerns, thereby providing partners with accessible

     6.   Product Liability
          (1) Customer Trust
               Autostreets has always regarded customer trust as the cornerstone of its growth, consistently
               delivering reliable services and exceptional experiences to every user. In 2025, the Company
               served customers on 60,000 occasions. Amid sustained business growth, it has consistently prioritized
               customer satisfaction, establishing a systematic customer feedback and response mechanism. Throughout
               the year, the Company received a total of 1,362 customer complaints, the majority of which pertained
               to vehicle condition. Through an efficient customer service mechanism and a dedicated resolution team,
               the Company achieved a complaint resolution rate of 99.00%. This not only demonstrates Autostreets’
               strong commitment to addressing customer concerns and its proactive responsiveness, but also effectively
               safeguards customer rights, further reinforcing client trust and the Company’s market reputation.

          (2)   Product Quality Control
                Autostreets has always regarded vehicle quality as the foundation of its business growth. By
                establishing a comprehensive end-to-end quality management system — spanning vehicle
                sourcing, professional inspection, and after-sales support — the Company ensures that every
                vehicle sold through the platform meets the highest standards. In 2025, Autostreets continued
                to expand its market transaction scale, achieving annual sales of over 140,000 gasoline vehicles, over
                1,000 diesel vehicles, and more than 9,000 new energy vehicles, with a total transaction value exceeding
                RMB4.246 billion. Amid rapid business growth, the Company maintained an outstanding service record
                of zero returns due to safety or quality issues throughout the year, underpinned by a rigorous vehicle
                admission mechanism, standardized inspection processes, and a professional after-sales service system.
                Specifically, we begin at the source by conducting compliance reviews of all documentation for vehicles
                entering the transaction process, ensuring they are legally eligible for trade. During the inspection phase,
                we leverage professional equipment and a skilled technical team to conduct systematic assessments of
                vehicle appearance, mechanical performance, and critical components, ensuring that vehicles are free from
                hidden defects and safety risks. After the transaction is completed, we continue to provide prompt and
                effective post-sale support to ensure the vehicle’s long-term reliability, thereby establishing a closed-loop
                quality assurance system that spans from vehicle onboarding to after-sales service.




84    Autostreets Development Limited / Annual Report 2025
ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND GOVERNANCE REPORT

    (3)   Vehicle Acquisition
          The used vehicle acquisition process is a critical component in ensuring vehicle quality and
          safeguarding brand reputation. Autostreets maintains rigorous, end-to-end oversight of this
          process to consistently deliver high-quality vehicles to its customers. Leveraging our proprietary
          EQS Inspection and Evaluation System, we complete a comprehensive vehicle assessment and issue an
          authoritative evaluation report in just 40 minutes. The system covers five major inspection categories
          and over 160 inspection points, with an added specialized module for the “three electric systems”–
          namely, the battery, motor, and power electronics — of new energy vehicles, ensuring full and gap-free
          coverage of all critical aspects. The report features clear and intuitive 3D damage visualizations, making
          the vehicle’s condition immediately apparent and providing robust support for the objective presentation
          of vehicle quality. Through systematic management of the vehicle acquisition process, Autostreets not
          only demonstrates its unwavering commitment to product quality but also significantly enhances customer
          confidence in vehicle purchases, showcasing its professionalism and sense of responsibility in the used car
          industry.

    (4)   Intellectual Property Management
          Intellectual property is a core competitive advantage for Autostreets and the fundamental
          safeguard underpinning its innovation-driven growth. The Company strictly complies with
          relevant laws and regulations, including the Patent Law, the Copyright Law, and the Trademark
          Law, actively safeguarding its own intellectual property rights while fully respecting the
          legitimate rights and interests of others. To this end, we have established an institutional framework
          centered on the Intellectual Property Management System, implementing end-to-end lifecycle management
          of intangible assets — including patents, trademarks, and software copyrights — spanning their creation,
          protection, and utilization to ensure the security and integrity of these assets.

          1)   Patent Management
               The Company has established a dedicated staff role staffed by personnel with specialized expertise
               in patents, who are responsible for the overall planning and implementation of intellectual property
               initiatives. This role is responsible for formulating the annual patent work plan, overseeing the patent
               application process, coordinating internal and external resources, and organizing internal training and
               awareness initiatives to continuously enhance company-wide understanding of patent protection and
               reinforce the strategic value of patents in driving innovation and competitive advantage.

          2)   Trademark Management
               Autostreets strictly complies with the Trademark Law in all aspects of trademark registration, use,
               and maintenance, adhering to standardized practices in daily operations to mitigate infringement
               risks. Upon discovering any trademark infringement, the Company will promptly take legal action
               — ranging from cease-and-desist letters to litigation — to firmly safeguard its brand reputation and
               market rights.




                                                            Autostreets Development Limited / Annual Report 2025   85
ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND GOVERNANCE REPORT

                3)    Software Copyright Management
                      The Company implements standardized archiving and dynamic monitoring for all software copyrights
                      that have been officially registered, ensuring the completeness and traceability of certificates and
                      related documentation. In the course of software usage, we strictly adhere to licensing terms to
                      mitigate infringement risks and enhance software protection through technical measures. Meanwhile,
                      the Company continuously monitors updates to relevant laws and regulations to ensure lawful and
                      compliant software operations.

                      As of 2025, Autostreets has been granted 8 invention patents and holds 54 registered software
                      copyrights. These achievements not only reflect the Company’s innovation capabilities but also
                      demonstrate its sustained investment in intellectual property strategy, laying a solid foundation for
                      long-term growth.

          (5)   Consumer Privacy Protection
                Autostreets has always placed consumer information security at the core of its operations, strictly
                adhering to laws and regulations such as the Cybersecurity Law and the Data Security Law. The
                Company implements privacy protection through a multi-dimensional approach that integrates
                institutional frameworks, process controls, rights safeguarding, and dynamic compliance. The
                Company has formulated and implemented internal policies, including the Information Asset Security
                Management Measures, the Privacy Rights Policy, and the Data Classification and Grading Guidelines, which
                clearly define categories of consumer personal information and establish comprehensive rules governing
                its handling throughout the entire data lifecycle. To operationalize these rules, Autostreets enforces strict
                access controls, encryption technologies, and authorization management, adheres to the principle of
                informed consent, and maintains rigorous oversight across all stages — from data collection, use, and
                sharing to deletion — ensuring that all operations are lawful, compliant, secure, and reliable.

                Meanwhile, the Company explicitly recognizes and safeguards consumers’ right to be informed and their
                right to make choices, providing clear and accessible channels for exercising data-related rights — including
                access, correction, and account deletion — and imposing no unreasonable restrictions. Personal information
                is not shared with external parties without the consumer’s explicit, separate consent, and any disclosure
                to third parties is strictly limited to the minimum necessary scope. Moreover, the Company has established
                a dynamic compliance mechanism: whenever business changes necessitate revisions to privacy policies,
                the business operations team and the information security team jointly conduct a review. This ensures
                continuous optimization of privacy protection strategies and the information security framework, reflecting
                the Company’s strong sense of responsibility in safeguarding consumer privacy and its commitment to
                building a transparent and trustworthy digital environment.

     7.   Anti-Corruption
          (1) Business Ethics
               Autostreets maintains a zero-tolerance policy toward any form of fraud, bribery, or corruption.
               Through a multi-pronged approach — including robust institutional frameworks, end-to-end
               process oversight, organization-wide training, diverse reporting channels, and strict disciplinary
               actions — the Company has established a sustainable, long-term mechanism for anti-fraud and
               anti-corruption governance. The Company strictly complies with national laws and regulations, including
               the Interim Provisions on the Prohibition of Commercial Bribery, and is firmly committed to upholding its
               corporate reputation, market integrity, and a fair, transparent, and trustworthy business environment.




86    Autostreets Development Limited / Annual Report 2025
ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND GOVERNANCE REPORT

          To implement this principle, the Company has established an institutional framework centered on the Anti-
          Bribery and Anti-Corruption Policy and the Anti-Fraud Management System, which clearly defines prohibited
          conduct, accountability mechanisms, and preventive controls; it continuously strengthens a closed-loop
          risk management system under the “three lines of defense” model, embedding anti-fraud requirements
          into all aspects of business operations, conducts specialized compliance and integrity risk audits at key
          auction venues — including Shanghai and Beijing — and actively drives corrective actions based on audit
          findings, while also enhancing employees’ legal awareness and professional ethics across the organization
          through policy communication, targeted training programs, and regular case-based alerts. Between 2022
          and 2025, the Company has handled relevant fraud-related personnel in strict accordance with laws and
          internal regulations, and issued company-wide email notifications detailing specific cases to deliver a strong
          deterrent effect.

    (2)   Whistle-blowing Mechanisms
          The Company upholds the principle of “Anti-corruption and Supervision by All,” and has
          established open, efficient, and responsive communication channels to receive reports and
          concerns from internal and external stakeholders — including employees and personnel from
          partner organizations — regarding violations of business ethics or anti-corruption requirements.
          The Company has formulated specialized management documents, including the Regulations for Enhancing
          the Code of Conduct for Auction Site Management, the Guidelines for Standardizing Auction Processes, and
          the Guidelines for Managing Key Indicators of Auction Sites, and has prominently displayed whistleblower
          and supervision notice signs at auction venues. To ensure that reports are handled efficiently, in accordance
          with established procedures, and with impartiality, the Company actively encourages named reporting.
          Employees, as well as individuals who have direct or indirect business relationships with the Company,
          may submit reports concerning fraudulent conduct to the Audit Department of the Company through
          multiple legitimate channels — including the dedicated whistleblower email (hr@autostreets.com), the
          hotline (400-821-8889), or in-person communication. Meanwhile, the Company places great emphasis on
          protecting the rights and interests of whistleblowers, and explicitly requires in its Anti-Fraud Management
          System that personnel handling anonymous reports must strictly maintain confidentiality and fulfill their
          duties with integrity throughout the entire process — including case intake, information registration,
          investigation and verification, and document custody. The Company has implemented multiple layers of
          security safeguards to fully ensure the confidentiality and security of whistleblowers’ personal information
          and report content, effectively mitigate the risk of data leakage or loss, and provide comprehensive
          protection of their rights, enabling anyone who reports suspected misconduct to do so without fear of
          retaliation.

     1.   Governance Structure

          Company has established a robust, powerful, and efficient governance structure as a tamped foundation for its
          sustainable development to secure steady progress in the fierce market competition, continuously create value for
          shareholders, customers, and society, and exhibit corporate responsibility and commitment.

          (1)   Composition of the Board of Directors

                for 33.33%. All members of the Board of Directors have a master’s degree or above, aged from 36 to 64,
                and a tiered structure of senior, middle-aged, and young members is set up on that basis, which has not
                only pooled together extensive experience, but injected innovation and vitality as well to provide a more
                comprehensive and forceful lens for the Company’s development. The members of the Board of Directors
                are equipped with diverse professional backgrounds covering a wide range of key areas such as finance,

                in decision-making, the Board of Directors has the capacity to provide the Company with comprehensive
                and professional guidance and effectively enhance corporate governance levels to secure solid intellectual

         (3)   ESG Governance of the Board of Directors
               Guided by China’s national “Carbon Peaking and Carbon Neutrality” strategy, the Company actively
               responds to global sustainability trends and continuously advances its green transformation and low-carbon
               development. Autostreets has placed the establishment of an ESG-related management committee on
               its strategic agenda for future development. Building on a robust sustainability organizational framework
               that vertically spans all auction sites and horizontally covers all functional departments, the Company
               deeply integrates ESG principles into every aspect of its operations. The Company aims to deeply embed
               sustainability into its corporate development through a systematic and institutionalized governance
               framework, demonstrating its commitment to social responsibility through concrete actions and steadily
               advancing toward a greener, more sustainable future.

               In 2025, the Company internally established a preliminary three-tier ESG structure:

               •     ESG Committee: Composed of senior executives from the Group’s leadership team, this committee
                     regularly reviews and provides recommendations on the Group’s sustainability initiatives, including
                     progress on major projects, annual operational targets, and the annual budget. It will provide far-
                     sighted recommendations and decisions for the Company’s sustainable development, ensuring
                     corporate development in synergy with the national “Carbon Peaking and Carbon Neutrality”
                     strategic goals and in step with the global trend of sustainable development.

               •     ESG Working Group: Comprised of managers from various functional departments across the
                     Group, this working group conducts internal discussions on matters including customer audits,
                     compliance, risk management, health and safety, and supplier management. Working group
                     members communicate regularly and report to the ESG Committee, ensuring that the ESG Committee
                     maintains real-time visibility into the detailed progress of the Company’s sustainability initiatives and
                     can make well-informed decisions.

               •     Daily Task Force: Composed of core staff from various business units, this team jointly drives and
                     coordinates the day-to-day advancement of sustainability initiatives across Autostreets. Through close
                     collaboration with the ESG Working Group, it provides timely feedback on issues and challenges in the
                     course of implementation, and at the same time actively implements the decisions and deployments
                     made by the ESG Committee and the ESG Working Group, so as to build a solid foundation for the
                     Company’s sustainable development.
